# MM6 3D model triangle-reduction ledger

Every installed 3D model that went through the reduction route ([RETOPOLOGY_PLAYBOOK.md](RETOPOLOGY_PLAYBOOK.md),
default route; decorations: [mm6_reduce_pilot](../decorations/mm6_reduce_pilot/README.md)). Update this table whenever
a reduced model is installed or rolled back. Triangle counts are full detail (LOD0) of the project export; the
installed runtime copy adds a few back-face copies.

| Model | File | Before | After | LODs 1/2/3 | Route | Meshy | Status | Project | Rollback source |

`mm6_gua_actor_models_bind_new_sorpigal_guards` and mage `mm6_pmn2_actor_models_bind_free_haven_mages`.

Decorations (2026-10-14): 18 often-placed MM6 decorations installed with reduced LOD0 (opaque faces only; leaf cards,
foam and fire untouched; existing LODs kept): trees 01/02/04/05/06/10/17/18/27/28/29, snow tree 01, surf rocks A/B,
rock01, cactus 25, burning rock, keg. Table, rollback sources (`backup_pre_reduce/`) and checks:
[mm6_reduce_pilot/README.md](../decorations/mm6_reduce_pilot/README.md).

## Far-LOD repair (2026-10-22)

LOD2 and LOD3 (and their shadow meshes) of 156 creature bodies rebuilt by [lod_repair_claude](lod_repair_claude/README.md):
MeshLab collapse of LOD0 (every LOD vertex an LOD0 vertex), new UVs (xatlas) and far textures filled from LOD0 in the
engine's normal-map frame; LOD0 and LOD1 unchanged, no Meshy. These counts supersede the "LODs 1/2/3" column above for
LOD2/LOD3 of the listed files. Kept as installed (already good, or worse when rebuilt): 29 models listed in
`lod_repair_claude/work/keep_list.txt`.

Region-mask alpha fix (2026-10-22): the far-LOD masks of `mm6_bld`, `mm6_fgt`, `mm6_hyd`, `mm6_mep`, `mm6_rob1`,
`mm7_m182`, `mm7_m461`, `mm8_m408`, `mm8_m480`, `mm8_m492` and `mm8_m564` were rebuilt with their alpha (fourth colour
region) and reinstalled; meshes and triangle counts below are unchanged.

## MM9 reduction (started 2026-11-04)

All MM9 models the user has approved (overview page `status:'ok'`), reduced on the playbook's no-retexture branch; brief and
rules: [REDUCTION_BRIEF_20261104.md](../../devdocs/mm9_rework/overnight/REDUCTION_BRIEF_20261104.md). One row per GLB, heaviest
first; small held props and hats (imp, guard, honk, boys' ear piece, lobber pod) are not listed. **This table is the record**:
update the row when a model starts, is ready for review, is installed (with the user's OK), is kept, or is rolled back.
Statuses: `queued` -> `reducing (<agent>, <date>)` -> `ready for review` -> `installed <date>` | `kept (no useful gain)` |
`rolled back`. Fre and the half-orc women join the queue once approved.

**Priority (user, 2026-11-04):** "the special/unique npcs - i dont care much about them, they can be higher tris. the monsters though, they need to be tris reduced since I will encounter a lot of them." ## MM9 far-LOD repair (2026-11-07)

The MM9 creatures were built with `tools/build_actor_lods.py` defaults after the 2026-10-22 far-LOD repair, so their
LOD3s are again broken (Sturmford City townswoman: floating sleeves, shattered skirt at the 72 px switch). An audit of
all 125 MM9 GLBs: 58 with a LOD3 outline error above 13 %. 94 bodies repaired by
[lod_repair_claude](lod_repair_claude/README.md) (section "MM9 run"); LOD0, LOD1 and clips unchanged, no Meshy. Kept as
installed: `mm9_skelwar` (repaired LOD2 worse), `mm9_fish` (transparent material, no merged shadow) and the attachments.

Variant far maps (2026-11-07): the far-LOD copies of tier materials with their own paint (or no region mask) pointed at
the default tier's far maps since the 2026-10-22 route; `scripts/fix_variant_maps.py` gave 43 installed GLBs their own
variant far maps (41 MM9, plus `mm7_m386` and `mm8_m580` from 2026-10-22); geometry and triangle counts unchanged.
The same day all 94 MM9 far texture sets were re-transferred (supersampled, UV correspondence; README "Grainy far
textures"); geometry and triangle counts unchanged.

## MM6–MM8 far-LOD repair (2026-11-12)

LOD2/LOD3 (and their shadow meshes) rebuilt from the **installed** GLBs by `lod_repair_claude/scripts/repair.py`
(UV-correspondence far maps with the facing check, 4x supersampling) per the [LOD playbook](LOD_PLAYBOOK.md), after the
[2026-11-11 LOD audit](audit_mm678_20261111/lod_audit/README.md); LOD0, LOD1, clips and materials unchanged
(`verify_far_only.py`), no Meshy. Rollback: `tools/install_model.py <rollback source> --world <w>`.
